### direct-es

```

```

### direct-zh

```

```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch onto the latest main to keep history clean before you open a PR. Merge when the branch is already shared or others have built on it, since rebasing would rewrite commits they depend on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径加在后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts
```

`bun test` 也接受文件名的一部分，比如 `bun test math` 会跑所有路径里带 `math` 的测试文件。

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "adds two numbers"
```

两者可以组合：`bun test test/math.test.ts -t "adds"`。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data, so a crash mid-write can be recovered by replaying the log. This lets the database stay durable and consistent without rewriting the full data on every change.
```

  [partner message] Done: the board is up with the two nights I have numbers for, and it'll fill in each night as the benchmark reports.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1999 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not run yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Writes per second, p99 latency</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
  <section class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </section>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var latestEl = document.getElementById('latest');
  var subEl = document.getElementById('sub');
  var listEl = document.getElementById('list');

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }

  function render(runs) {
    runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
    if (!runs.length) {
      latestEl.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">Not run yet</span>';
      subEl.textContent = 'Writes per second, p99 latency';
      listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">Each night’s run lands here.</div>';
      return;
    }
    var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    latestEl.textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
    subEl.textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · ' + last.night;
    listEl.innerHTML = runs.slice().reverse().map(function (r) {
      var stalls = (r.stalls === undefined || r.stalls === null) ? '' : ' · ' + r.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
      return '<div class="k-row">' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(r.night) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + esc(r.p99) + stalls + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + esc(r.writes) + ' /s</div>' +
      '</div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  var stored = ducter.get('runs');
  if (stored && typeof stored.then === 'function') {
    stored.then(render);
  } else {
    render(stored);
  }
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    var v = ducter.get('runs');
    if (v && typeof v.then === 'function') { v.then(render); } else { render(v); }
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms"},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

### app-make-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 给我做个小应用，记一下这趟北京出差的花销，按天列，带个合计
  [partner message] 做好了，每天一块，记一笔就自动加总。你告诉我每笔花了什么、多少钱，我帮你填进去。
  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3154 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">0 笔 · 0 天</div>
  </div>
  <div id="days"></div>
  <button class="k-btn" onclick="addDay()" style="width:100%;margin-top:12px">+ 加一天</button>
</main>
<script>
let S = { days: [] };
let sel = null;

const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const fmt = n => '¥' + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toFixed(2);

function render() {
  let total = 0, count = 0;
  const box = document.getElementById('days');
  box.innerHTML = S.days.map((d, di) => {
    const sum = d.items.reduce((a, i) => a + i.amount, 0);
    total += sum; count += d.items.length;
    const rows = d.items.length
      ? d.items.map((it, ii) => {
          const key = di + '-' + ii;
          const row = '<div class="k-row" onclick="pick(\'' + key + '\')">'
            + '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(it.what) + '</div>'
            + '<div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(it.amount) + '</div></div>';
          return row + (sel === key ? '<button class="k-b … ss="k-empty">还没有一天。点下面加一天，按天记。</div>';

  document.getElementById('total').innerHTML = count > 0
    ? fmt(total)
    : '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = count + ' 笔 · ' + S.days.length + ' 天';
}

function save() {
  ducter.set('trip', S);
  const total = S.days.reduce((a, d) => a + d.items.reduce((b, i) => b + i.amount, 0), 0);
  const count = S.days.reduce((a, d) => a + d.items.length, 0);
  ducter.summary(count + ' 笔,合计 ' + fmt(total));
  render();
}

function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trip')).then(v => {
    if (v && v.days) S = v;
    render();
  });
}

function addDay() {
  S.days.push({ name: '第' + (S.days.length + 1) + '天', items: [] });
  save();
}

function addItem(di) {
  const w = document.getElementById('w' + di).value.trim();
  const a = parseFloat(document.getElementById('a' + di).value);
  if (!w || !(a > 0)) return;
  S.days[di].items.push({ what: w, amount: Math.round(a * 100) / 100 });
  save();
}

function pick(key) {
  sel = sel === key ? null : key;
  render();
}

function delItem(di, ii) {
  S.days[di].items.splice(ii, 1);
  sel = null;
  save();
}

render();
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

})();

  <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
</div>
</main>
<script>
const PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
let landed = [];

function esc(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c]));
}

function render() {
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = PRS.map(p => {
    const done = landed.includes(p.id);
    return '<li class="k-row" data-id="' + p.id + '" role="button" aria-pressed="' + done + '" style="cursor:pointer">' +
      '<div class="k-row-main"><div>' + (done ? "✓ " : "") + '<strong>' + esc(p.title) + '</strong> · ' + esc(p.what) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(done ? "Landed" : p.status) + '</div></div>' +
      '</li>';
  }).join("");
  const left = PRS.length - landed.length;
  document.getElementById("left").textContent = String(left);
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = landed.length ? landed.length + " landed" : "tap a PR when it lands";
  if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(left + " waiting");
}

function apply(v) {
  try { landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : JSON.parse(v || "[]"); } catch (e) { landed = []; }
  render();
}

list.addEventListener && 0;
document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
  const row = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("[data-id]");
  if (!row) return;
  const id = row.getAttribute("data-id");
  landed = landed.includes(id) ? landed.filter(x => x !== id) : landed.concat(id);
  ducter.set("landed", landed);
  render();
});

if (window.ducter) {
  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () {
    const v = ducter.get("landed");
    if (v && typeof v.then === "function") v.then(apply); else apply(v);
  });
  const v = ducter.get("landed");
  if (v && typeof v.then === "function") v.then(apply); else apply(v);
} else {
  render();
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412","what":"Compaction cut","status":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Mon"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","what":"Retry on 502","status":"CI failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","status":"Waiting on Alice since Tue"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418","what":"Apps bar","status":"Draft"}]}

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 卡通笔记本电脑：深藏青色的边框，屏幕是空白的，键盘和触控板都很清楚，背景是透明的。

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好表示"这里还没有内容"，画风柔和，不会让人觉得出错了。用的时候注意几点：

- 它本身不带文字，要配一句说明，比如"还没有记录"，再配一个操作按钮。
- 320×246 不算大，展示时别缩得太小，屏幕上的细节会糊掉。
- 深色模式下，深藏青的边框可能和背景融在一起，最好测一下，必要时加一点底色或者换成浅色版本。
```
